# Gig: Heaviest of Hearts

Journal: `ep1/quests/street_stories/sts_ep1_06` · `StreetStory` · hash `2111011406`.

Journal premise: Gig objective: Carry out the assignment from Michael Maldonado\n\nLocation: Golden Pacific\n\nDetails:\nI have been informed that it is a delicate matter, despite how mundane it sounds. Michael Maldonado has been blessed with two things that go together many a time: a son and trouble. He will inform you of the exact nature of assistance he requires. All I can tell you at this point is that Michael is willing to compensate you – and me – surprisingly well for the bother. Do visit him to learn more about the nature of the job. Maldonado runs a business he seems to never leave unattended. You know how it is, V – those that possess very little are the most afraid to lose whatever they have.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 102 exact journal-linked nodes in 2 resources; 14 resources in the inspected dependency context.

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

832 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 172 distinct direct resource identities; 39 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

## Scene performance events

Section event data retains actor/animation/audio bindings and authored time fields, with raw pointers. Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
